What to do
----------
  1. Look at each of those 4356 transitions in IDEN2 and settle it, recording the verdict in line_decisions.csv; where it is the share of a blend that differs, correct the weight in LOPT_input_lines.txt by hand.  Do not simply re-run make_LOPT_input.py: that rebuilds LOPT_input_lines.txt from the current classification, so the list above disappears while every question in it stays open and is now untracked.
  2. Re-run lopt.bat on the current LOPT_input_lines.txt.
  3. Withdraw those 3 assignments in IDEN2, or accept them in the classification: at present the screen and the fit disagree.
  4. Carry those 2 new assignments into IDEN2 so that the screen shows the fit that is actually being used.
  5. Re-run level_positions.py (and level_shifts.py) last: their reports are built on everything above, and a report made before the fixes will recommend things that have already been done.

  They are in the order the pipeline runs.  Doing a later
  one first only means doing it again after the earlier one.
